**0.12.81.365 candidate — FIRST-TOUCH-DIRECTIONAL-CUSHION-01 (27 November 2026):** FIRST-TOUCH-DIRECTIONAL-CUSHION-01: during the existing pressured low-ground-pass settling window, steering away from the nearest active grounded outfield defender now restores up to 8% movement speed, scaled by remaining settle time, pressure and Dribbling. Steering into pressure or perpendicular to the escape lane receives no bonus; downed, stunned, invulnerable, airborne, recovering, distant and missing defenders do not create an escape bonus. The base settling penalty and timer, ball physics, kicks, tackles, four actions, saves, rosters, persistence and offline play remain unchanged. Mirrored Node and actual-GameEngine desktop/simulated-touch Chromium regressions cover both teams, skill scaling and inactive-defender fallbacks.

### 27 November 2026 — FIRST-TOUCH-DIRECTIONAL-CUSHION-01 (smf-hourly-dev-20261019)
**Acceptance:** For both teams, during the existing pressured friendly low-ground-pass settling window, a receiver steering away from the nearest active grounded outfield defender must regain more movement speed than the same receiver steering toward that defender. The escape cushion is capped at 8 percentage points at 100 Dribbling and scales with normalized Dribbling, remaining timer fraction and existing first-touch pressure. Perpendicular/neutral input, no pressure, no active defender within 135 units, expired settling, or downed, stunned, invulnerable, airborne and recovery-state opponents must add no bonus. The total scale never exceeds normal movement. Preserve the existing 0.18–0.30-second duration, base skill/pressure penalty, player direction, ball flight, kicks, tackles, four actions, saves, rosters, persistence and offline play.
**Implementation:** moveAgile now compares the live movement input with the away vector from the nearest eligible defender while the existing first-touch timer is active. Only positive away alignment contributes an additional remaining-time × pressure × Dribbling × 0.08 movement-scale cushion; all other cases use the prior scale. No new player state, timer, ball impulse, collision or save field is introduced.
**Regression:** Extended first_touch_control.test.cjs with exact 8%-maximum and Dribbling-scaled boundaries plus actual moveAgile comparisons for away/toward/perpendicular input, no defender and downed/stunned/invulnerable/keeper/airborne/recovery/invalid-coordinate fallbacks. Extended first_touch_control_browser_qa.py to verify the real GameEngine movement difference in desktop and simulated-touch matches while preserving immediate shot, pass event, score/team/formation and four-action checks.

**0.12.81.349 candidate — PASS-LANE-AGILITY-STEERING-READ-01 (27 November 2026):** PASS-LANE-AGILITY-STEERING-READ-01: directed ground-pass safety now projects an active defender through the same bounded Agility-limited turn response used by CPU movement before scoring marker and interception pressure. Also repaired three .337 isolated-source test contracts: the Hide Ball poke expression now receives its actual counter set, and Fireball/Scream source contracts recognize the expanded Hide Ball counter membership.

**0.12.81.336 candidate — KEEPER-PARRY-REACHABLE-RUNNER-READ-01 (26 November 2026):** goalkeeper parry-lane selection now includes an attacker who begins just outside the 360-unit current scan but can enter the rebound corridor within the existing ±150-unit-per-axis movement projection. **0.12.81.335 candidate — KEEPER-PARRY-TURN-AWARE-LANE-READ-01 (26 November 2026):** KEEPER-PARRY-TURN-AWARE-LANE-READ-01: goalkeeper parry selection now projects active attackers through the existing Agility-limited directional turn response when evaluating the two rebound corridors. An elite runner who can turn into the preferred side makes the keeper choose the opposite, clearer lane; a low-Agility runner who retains momentum away no longer falsely blocks the original side. The existing 0.24/0.48-second samples, 0.18-second turn phase, ±150-unit per-axis lead cap, active/recovery filters, 24-unit lane margin, rebound speed and magnitude, catch threshold and dive physics remain unchanged. Neutral/invalid input and slow movement retain the exact linear fallback. Mirrored Node and actual-engine desktop/simulated-touch Chromium regressions cover both goals and high/low Agility.
### 26 November 2026 — KEEPER-PARRY-TURN-AWARE-LANE-READ-01 (smf-hourly-dev-20261019)
**Acceptance:** For both defending goals, when an active attacker is moving away from a rebound corridor but its live directional input can turn it into that corridor before the parry arrives, the keeper must account for the existing Agility-limited turn response. High Agility must make the preferred lane look occupied and select the clearer opposite side; low Agility must retain enough of its existing momentum away that the preferred lane remains available. Preserve the existing 0.24/0.48-second samples, 0.18-second turn phase, ±150-unit per-axis cap, active/recovery exclusions, 24-unit lane margin, shot-angle fallback, rebound velocity magnitude, catch threshold, dive/recovery and actual ball contact. Neutral/invalid input and slow movement must retain the exact linear projection. Keep four actions, saves, rosters, formations, persistence and offline play unchanged.
**Implementation:** keeperParrySide now projects each active outfielder through the shared defensiveCarrierTurnVelocity helper. It integrates the bounded turn phase with a trapezoidal start/end velocity average, then continues at the turned velocity for the remaining sample interval. When the shared helper returns unchanged velocity, the previous linear projection is used verbatim.
**Regression:** Extended keeper_parry_lane.test.cjs to use the production Agility-turn helper and cover mirrored elite/low-Agility attackers plus neutral/invalid fallback. Extended actual-engine desktop/simulated-touch keeper_parry_lane_browser_qa.py to call tryKeeperDive on both goals and assert real rebound direction for elite, low-Agility and neutral-input cases while retaining the existing recovery and four-button checks.
**Release-gate status at commit:** Exact-head Modular QA and four-Node/four-Chromium Full Regression Census triggered by this commit; results must be inspected before any promotion.

**0.12.81.317 release candidate (26 November 2026):** FIREBALL-READY-TARGET-RESOLVER-PARITY-02: Fireball, Freeze and Bat now resolve against the same ready-defender set used by CPU wind-up reactions. ### 26 November 2026 — FIREBALL-READY-TARGET-RESOLVER-PARITY-02 (smf-hourly-dev-20261019)
**Acceptance:** When the nearest opponent is unavailable during Fireball, Freeze or Bat, the actual trick resolver must select the same nearest ready defender used by the CPU wind-up reaction. Downed, invulnerable, shot-impact pending/knocked-down/rising, shotDown/get-up, hurt-recovering, early JUMP below 20 units, invalid-height and invalid-coordinate defenders cannot absorb the target while an active teammate remains in the existing range. Preserve all existing trick ranges, close-slide timing, direct and screen reads, cooldown/fatigue, four actions, saves, rosters, persistence and offline play.
**Implementation:** tryMagicTrick now applies one shared readyCounterTargets filter to Fireball, Freeze, Bat and Scream before choosing the resolver rival. This matches reactToFireballTrick availability checks; Lightning keeps its dedicated delayed-target filter, and unrelated tricks retain the legacy rival pool.
**Regression:** Added a focused Node source-contract assertion and retained the actual-engine desktop/simulated-touch browser handoff matrix for both teams and Fireball/Freeze/Bat. The browser test verifies the second defender reaches JUMP counter height before the cast and that the resolver emits countered: true while the nearer defender remains invulnerable.

### 26 November 2026 — FIREBALL-READY-DEFENDER-PARITY-01 (smf-hourly-dev-20261019)

**0.12.81.315 release candidate (26 November 2026):** SCREAM-READY-DEFENDER-HANDOFF-01: CPU Scream counterplay now skips a nearer opponent who is down, invulnerable, in shot-impact/hurt recovery or still below the JUMP counter height, then hands the visible one-second warning to the next ready defender. ### 26 November 2026 — SCREAM-READY-DEFENDER-HANDOFF-01 (smf-hourly-dev-20261019)
**Acceptance:** During the existing visible Scream hold, the nearest opponent is a valid response target only if active, non-invulnerable, in a valid height state, not down or in shot-impact/shot-down/get-up/hurt recovery, and not in an early JUMP below the existing 20-unit dodge height. If unavailable, CPU reaction selects the nearest next ready teammate within 135 units using the same difficulty delay. At cast resolution, Scream evaluates the same ready-target set so the handed-off defender's active JUMP (z >= 20) or grounded SHOOT-slide counters; an already-effective nearest JUMP/slide retains priority. Preserve the one-second DRIBBLE hold, Defence-versus-Dribbling direct read, screen read, six-second cooldown, fatigue, four actions, saves, rosters, persistence and offline play.
**Implementation:** `reactToScreamTrick` filters downed, invulnerable, shot-impact pending/knocked-down/rising, shot-down/get-up, hurt-recovering, invalid-height and not-yet-airborne jump candidates before selecting the nearest CPU responder. `tryMagicTrick` uses the matching Scream-only target filter for its rival, direct read and screen read. Other Magic Tricks retain their prior rival pool.
**Regression:** Mirrored Node tests cover unavailable-nearest handoff, no-ready fallback, already-effective JUMP/slide and actual resolver selection. The desktop/simulated-touch actual-engine browser test drives the second defender through `GameEngine.driveAI`, casts Scream and asserts the handed-off JUMP is recognized, with four-action checks.

**0.12.81.314 release candidate (26 November 2026):** AI-THROUGH-BALL-DEFENDER-TURN-SAFETY-01: CPU through-ball collection-pocket safety now integrates active defenders' current directional input through the existing Agility-limited turn response over the same bounded pass-arrival estimate. A high-Agility defender turning into the receiver's pocket can now close a pass that straight-line momentum would incorrectly leave open; a lower-Agility defender retains more residual momentum and does not falsely block it. The existing 65-unit per-axis projection cap, goalkeeper reach, lane-pressure calculation, receiver lead, pass physics, four actions, saves, rosters, persistence and offline play remain unchanged. Mirrored Node and actual-engine desktop/simulated-touch Chromium regressions cover both goals, high/low Agility and neutral/invalid-input fallback.

### 26 November 2026 — AI-THROUGH-BALL-DEFENDER-TURN-SAFETY-01 (smf-hourly-dev-20261019)
**Acceptance:** Keep the existing through-ball candidate gates, receiver projection, pass-arrival estimate, 65-unit per-axis defender projection cap, lane-pressure calculation, goalkeeper collection reach and 84-unit outfield collection radius. For each active grounded defender, integrate current movement toward live dx/dy through the shared Agility-limited turn response in bounded 180 ms segments; average start/end velocity per segment and compare the capped projected endpoint with the receiver's collection pocket. High Agility turning into the pocket must block a pass that linear momentum alone leaves open; low Agility must retain enough residual momentum to leave the same pocket available. Neutral/invalid input, slow defenders, missing/invalid Agility, keeper reach, lane pressure, receiver lead, pass physics, four actions, saves, rosters, persistence and offline play remain compatible.
**Implementation:** `attackingThroughBall` projects each eligible defender with `carrierTurnAwareVelocity` before the existing collection-radius comparison. It preserves the 65-unit per-axis cap and all readiness, stun, flight-time and goalkeeper checks.
**Regression:** Repaired the .313 isolated Node fixture to use actual `GameEngine` players (the prior test referenced an undefined `player()` factory). Added mirrored high/low-Agility defender-turn, neutral/invalid fallback cases. Actual-engine desktop/simulated-touch acceptance is added in the matching browser fixture.


### 26 November 2026 — AI-THROUGH-BALL-AGILITY-TURN-LEAD-01 (smf-hourly-dev-20261019)
**Acceptance:** Keep the existing committed-runner gates (grounded/ready state, 90–880-unit carrier range, 90–760-unit forward lane, 118–280-unit support gap, raw forward run speed at least 110 and forward velocity at least 65), the 0.12–0.35 s pass-flight estimate, 48–100-unit longitudinal lead, ±32-unit lateral lead, 24-unit playable-field margin and all defender/keeper corridor and collection-pocket checks. Integrate the receiver's current velocity toward live dx/dy through the existing Agility-limited turn response in bounded 180 ms segments, averaging start/end velocity per segment. Project the resulting displacement onto the planned forward and lateral axes before applying the unchanged lead clamps. High Agility must follow a sharp cut further than low Agility; neutral/invalid input retains the prior residual-velocity lead and missing/invalid Agility defaults to 50. Keep support-run selection, through-ball action, ball/pass physics, four actions, possession, saves, rosters, persistence and offline play unchanged.
**Implementation:** attackingThroughBall now integrates carrierTurnAwareVelocity over the existing flight estimate and derives both forward and lateral lead from the projected displacement. The existing raw movement gate remains authoritative, while neutral/invalid input retains the prior trajectory.
**Regression:** Added mirrored Node cases for high/average/low Agility turn ordering, missing/invalid ratings, neutral/invalid directional input and unchanged fallback. Extended actual-engine desktop/simulated-touch Chromium acceptance to execute the through-ball decision for both teams and verify the resulting pass direction follows the Agility-scaled cut while preserving the 223-team catalog and exactly four action buttons.

**Acceptance:** For a grounded opponent carrier already moving at least 75 units/s, use its current normalized 2D input to anticipate a turn over the same bounded 80–180 ms read horizon used by the primary press lead. Low Agility therefore retains more residual momentum; high Agility can reach a sharp cut lane sooner.

**Acceptance:** Lightning must lock onto the nearest active opponent within its existing 95-unit range, skipping downed, invulnerable, shot-impact-pending/knocked-down/rising, shot-down/get-up and hurt-recovery defenders. **Acceptance:** When a CPU SHOOT slide has selected the opponent's validated exposed carried ball, preserve the 85 ms average-carrier lead but scale it by the carrier's existing Agility: 100 => 70 ms, 50 => 85 ms, 1 => 99.7 ms, clamped to 70–100 ms. **0.12.81.304 release candidate (25 November 2026):** AI-DRIBBLE-RESIDUAL-CLOSING-READ-01: dribble-trick duel odds now use the nearest defender's actual residual world velocity (moveVX/moveVY, with legacy velocity and direction fallbacks) rather than normalized directional input. A defender still closing after releasing the stick or keys therefore receives the existing bounded read bonus and Rainbow counter; stationary or retreating markers do not. The duel radius, style/stat/fatigue formula, escape movement, multi-defender lane choice, four actions, saves, rosters, persistence and offline play remain unchanged. ### 25 November 2026 — AI-DRIBBLE-RESIDUAL-CLOSING-READ-01 (smf-hourly-dev-20261019)
**Acceptance:** When resolving a dribble-trick duel, supply the nearest eligible defender's actual world movement velocity to the existing dribbleOdds spatial read. Residual movement after input release must still count as closing; stationary and retreating defenders must not receive the close-pressure, Nutmeg or Rainbow counter bonuses. Preserve current player positions, duel radius, stats, fatigue/style coefficients and probability bounds, escape side/trajectory, multi-defender lane comparison, human/CPU action mapping, four actions, saves, rosters, persistence and offline play.
**Implementation:** The engine now passes moveVX/moveVY to dribbleOdds, falls back to legacy physical vx/vy, then reconstructs world velocity from normalized dx/dy and the defender's existing playerSpeed. No odds coefficients or movement/physics values changed.
**Regression:** Added mirrored real-engine Node cases where the same Rainbow duel succeeds against a stationary marker but is countered by a marker still closing after input release; extended desktop/simulated-touch Chromium acceptance for both teams and updated the source contract to require physical velocity wiring. Exact-head Modular QA and four-Node/four-Chromium Census required; .304 is not promoted or deployed until green.

**0.12.81.303 release candidate (25 November 2026):** AI-DRIBBLE-TRICK-MULTI-DEFENDER-ESCAPE-LANE-01: CPU-controlled dribble tricks now score both lateral exit lanes against additional ready defenders' bounded 120 ms projected positions instead of always escaping from only the nearest marker. A second defender actively closing the nearest-away route can therefore redirect the trick into the safer lane; active slides use their remaining swept travel, and near-equal risks retain the original nearest-defender choice. The nearest defender still determines duel odds, while human trick movement, ten styles, stamina/cooldowns, ball physics, four actions, saves, rosters, persistence and offline play remain unchanged. Mirrored Node and actual-engine desktop/simulated-touch Chromium regressions cover both attack directions, a second defender closing the preferred exit, active-slide arrival and unchanged human behavior.

### 25 November 2026 — AI-DRIBBLE-TRICK-MULTI-DEFENDER-ESCAPE-LANE-01 (smf-hourly-dev-20261019)
**Acceptance:** When a CPU carrier attempts a lateral dribble trick under pressure, keep the nearest ready defender as the duel/odds authority and baseline escape side. If an additional ready defender is actively closing that nearest-away route within the next 120 ms, redirect the CPU escape toward the lower-risk lateral lane. Stationary or retreating secondary defenders must not rewrite the previous side choice; active slides use only their remaining valid swept travel. Human-controlled trick direction, all ten style profiles, success odds, cooldowns, stamina, possession, ball physics, four actions, saves, rosters, persistence and offline play remain unchanged.
**Implementation:** Added a bounded two-lane risk comparison for CPU tricks only. Additional defenders contribute only when their projected path or endpoint gap closes by at least six units; movement lead is capped at 32 units, active slides use remaining timed travel, and a two-point risk margin preserves the existing nearest-defender choice when lanes are effectively tied.
**Regression:** Extended the existing ready-threat Node and actual-engine desktop/simulated-touch Chromium suites for both attack directions, a second defender moving into the nearest-away lane, an active slide reaching that lane, and unchanged human movement. Exact-head Modular QA and four-Node/four-Chromium Census required; .303 is not promoted or deployed until green.

**0.12.81.302 release candidate (25 November 2026):** KEEPER-SHOT-LANE-BRAKING-READ-01: CPU attackers now estimate an opposing goalkeeper's likely lateral position at shot arrival with a bounded braking read instead of assuming current movement continues unchanged through the whole flight. The existing 0.10–0.34 s horizon and 65-unit lead cap remain; braking authority blends the keeper's Agility and GK Skill, while fatigue reduces it, so a fresh high-skill keeper sheds more cross-lane drift and a tired or low-skill keeper remains committed longer. This changes only CPU shot-lane selection; human aim, keeper movement/dive/catch, shot and ball physics, four actions, saves, rosters, persistence and offline play remain unchanged. ### 25 November 2026 — KEEPER-SHOT-LANE-BRAKING-READ-01 (smf-hourly-dev-20261019)
**Acceptance:** For a CPU-controlled shot, project the opposing keeper's lateral position over the existing 0.10–0.34 second shot-flight horizon while allowing a bounded portion of current cross-lane velocity to brake. Blend braking authority from the keeper's existing Agility and GK Skill; fatigue reduces braking so a tired keeper remains committed longer. Preserve the existing ±65-unit projection cap, goalmouth clamps, recovery-state no-projection behavior, human shot aim, keeper movement/dive/catch, ball/shot physics, four actions, saves, rosters, persistence and offline play.
**Implementation:** keeperShotReadY now applies a bounded constant-deceleration estimate: braking rate is 250–750 world units/s² from 55% Agility and 45% GK Skill, reduced by up to 40% at full fatigue. The helper projects no farther than the existing 65-unit cap and never reverses current lateral movement. Only the CPU shoot lane resolver consumes this estimate.
**Regression:** Extended ai_keeper_shot_read.test.cjs for low/neutral/elite ratings, rested/tired ordering, mirrored velocity, missing-rating fallback, legacy velocity and cap. Extended keeper_shot_calibration_browser_qa.py to exercise the actual GameEngine.shoot path in desktop and simulated-touch Chromium for both teams, mirrored keeper movement, human aim isolation, 223-team catalog and four actions. Exact-head Modular QA and four-Node/four-Chromium Census required; .302 is not promoted or deployed.

Downed, stunned, airborne, sliding, hurt-recovering and shot-impact-recovering defenders must not suppress the read. If another runner remains open, select that alternative. Preserve existing runner scoring, keeper skill-scaled shade, goal bounds, dive/catch/shot physics, four actions, saves, rosters, persistence and offline play.
**Implementation:** `keeperCutbackReadLane` now projects eligible defending outfielders with the existing `moveVX/moveVY` and legacy `vx/vy` fallback, then excludes only runner candidates whose projected marker gap is below 64 units. Existing active-state checks are shared; no keeper movement or contact physics changed.
**Regression:** Extended `keeper_cutback_read.test.cjs` with mirrored ready-marker, hurt-recovery and open-alternative cases. Extended `keeper_cutback_runner_arrival_browser_qa.py` for desktop and simulated touch across both goals and both cutback wings, preserving 223 teams and exactly four action buttons.

> **0.12.81.293 release candidate (25 November 2026):** AI-DEFENSIVE-PRESS-CROSS-TRACK-TURN-READ-01: CPU primary/support role selection now adds a bounded 120 ms cross-track turn reserve when evaluating a projected carrier or cover point. Lateral momentum that must be redirected consumes up to 12 units of the short arrival score; the reserve is scaled by the defender's existing Agility, so higher-Agility players reorient sooner while lower-Agility players are more conservative. Aligned, retreating and stationary movement retain their existing arrival-projection behavior. This changes role choice only, not player speed, movement physics, tackle contact/probability, possession, four actions, saves, rosters, persistence or offline play. Mirrored Node and actual-engine desktop/simulated-touch Chromium regressions cover both teams, low/average/high Agility, cross-track versus aligned movement and primary assignment.

### 2026-11-25 — AI-DEFENSIVE-PRESS-CROSS-TRACK-TURN-READ-01 (smf-hourly-dev-20261019)
**Acceptance:** When CPU pressing or a validated loose-ball support assignment evaluates a projected carrier/cover point, cross-track momentum must consume a bounded portion of the short arrival score instead of being treated as immediately redirectable. The reserve is scaled by the defender's existing Agility (default 50, clamped 1–100), capped at 12 units, and applies only to lateral velocity relative to the evaluated lane. Aligned movement keeps its existing arrival benefit; retreating movement remains governed by the existing projected-distance score; stationary and malformed movement retain the previous baseline. Preserve the 120 ms projection, ±24-unit arrival delta and 0.55 weight, 220-unit/s projection cap, support locality/flank rules, human exclusions, tackle/contact, possession, ball physics, four actions, saves, rosters, persistence and offline play.
**Implementation:** defensivePressers now computes a 120 ms Agility-scaled cross-track turn reserve from current velocity and the projected press/support point. The reserve uses the same 3 + Agility × 0.13 turn-rate response as moveAgile, is weighted by 0.45 and capped at 12 score units. It is added only to projected primary/support arrival scores; player movement and all existing distance/cover geometry remain unchanged.
**Regression:** Extended ai_defensive_press_arrival.test.cjs with mirrored low/average/high/invalid Agility cross-track-versus-steady arrival ties, capped extreme momentum and actual GameEngine.driveAI assignment/possession checks. Extended desktop/simulated-touch Chromium acceptance for both defending directions; the 223-team catalog, exactly-four-action HUD and page-error checks remain.


**0.12.81.293 first exact-head preflight diagnosis (25 November 2026):** Modular QA #45603566184 and Full Regression Census #45603565956 both stopped before feature tests because the first source edit replaced only the defensivePressers signature and left the previous function body after the new closing brace. Node module parsing reported SyntaxError: Unexpected token 'function' at defensiveCounterpressTrapTarget; the new cross-track logic was not runtime-tested. The follow-up replaces the complete defensivePressers block through its next function boundary, preserving the original role-selection logic plus the bounded Agility turn reserve. Fresh exact-head Modular QA and Full Regression Census are required.

### 2026-11-25 — AI-MAGIC-TRICK-ACTIVE-APPROACH-READ-01 (smf-hourly-dev-20261019)
**Acceptance:** A direct named Magic Trick read is valid only for an active grounded defender with no down/invulnerability/slide/hurt/shot-impact recovery and a meaningful approach vector (cosine at least 0.22) toward the carrier. A grazing lateral path, stationary facing, retreat, airborne or recovering defender must not counter. A genuine directional-input or residual-momentum approach still counters. Dedicated JUMP and grounded SHOOT-slide trick counters remain separate; preserve cooldown/fatigue costs, ball ownership, visible cue, four actions, saves, rosters, persistence and offline play.
**Implementation:** GameEngine.tryMagicTrick now rejects unavailable direct readers and requires the normalized approach alignment to exceed 0.22, scaling the dot-product boundary by both approach distance and heading magnitude. Existing input-first/residual-momentum fallback and Defence-versus-Dribbling comparison remain.
**Regression:** Extended magic_trick_secondary_screen_read.test.cjs and its actual-engine desktop/simulated-touch Chromium acceptance with mirrored glancing-angle and downed, invulnerable, airborne, slide/hurt and shot-impact recovery cases. Existing approach, retreat, stationary, second-defender screen, JUMP/SHOOT-slide counters, 223-team catalog and exactly-four-action checks remain.

# SuperMagicFootball — master plan and backlog

**Canonical plan and status ledger. Updated 27 November 2026 for candidate 0.12.81.354; exact-head gates pending. Current dev branch is on candidate 0.12.81.354; main remains `f83d578e597703eee7ff2f958b0d971aa92e81a6` (0.12.81.349). Candidate .353 is not certified, promoted or deployed. The last recorded successful main .349 Pages deployment and independent Census are #45932990259 and #45932996471; no .353 exact-head Census/Modular QA result is available from the connected run lookup.

## Owner priorities and invariants

- Prioritize playable gameplay and abilities, existing in-game player graphics/animation, VFX/stadium/pitch, responsive/accessibile UI, Career/Cup, and online functionality.
- Keep the existing Home/menu character art and supplied reference layout; the owner said that art is good and asked that effort go to gameplay and ball/player visuals.
- Defer player/team identity audits, roster validation and player-stat tuning. Skip new Concept Hero/Royal Concept teams and all new concept-hero creation.
- Preserve offline play, save compatibility, roster identities/order, six formations, keyboard/touch/gamepad access, accessibility and the existing four-action input schema.
- SuperMagicAssetStudio is the source-art tool. The game consumes only approved exported assets/manifests; keep the procedural renderer as fallback. Structural checks do not imply owner visual approval.
- Do not claim visual approval, physical-device acceptance, protected promotion, hosting or server readiness without evidence.

## Previous promoted candidate 0.12.81.48

The production online-account HTTP bridge now allowlists the exact authenticated `/api/v1/ultimate-team/lineup` and `/api/v1/online-career/seasons` paths. The adapter forwards lineup GET/PUT bodies and responses and the archive GET response while continuing to reject unknown nested routes. Existing authentication, bounded streaming, timeout and response-header rules remain in place. This fixes an integration 404 in the real Node service; it does not imply public hosting or a live PostgreSQL setup. Focused HTTP tests passed **14/14**.

Candidate .48 build passed (**5,178,925 JS / 5,301,937 HTML characters**); full Node passed **2,245/2,245** plus Club26/FC27. The focused HTTP adapter suite passed **14/14** and release/version contracts **16/16**; `git diff --check` is clean. Candidate .48 SHA `461b2a7b934862e753d854ea9f0bc4e293031ca1` passed exact-head Modular QA and Full Regression Census, was promoted to main, deployed via Pages, and passed main Census. See [candidate .48 validation](CANDIDATE_012848_VALIDATION_20261105.md).

## Previous promoted candidate 0.12.81.49

The existing procedural renderer gives stationary players two staggered pupil-only idle glances with broad neutral rests. Sclera, lids, brows and face geometry do not move; gaze is disabled for motion/action, hurt, crying and reduced-flash. No player/ball simulation state or timers change. Preview capture: [idle gaze actual-renderer samples](evidence/browser/player_idle_gaze_012849.png); this automated crop is not human or owner visual approval.

Online API startup now retains a PostgreSQL session advisory lock before runtime/listener setup. A contender fails startup before listening; loss of the lock connection closes transport/matchmaking/listener and readiness, preventing split process-local matches. After this lock is acquired, startup reconciles reserved Career fixtures in bounded batches: only immutable durable results advance through the normal idempotent path; reservations without a final result become retryable without score or rewards. Online UT lineup writes now share an account-level advisory transaction lock consistent with the account-scoped idempotency ledger key, preventing same-key requests for different teams from racing into a raw database uniqueness error.

Candidate .49 build/full Node/data checks passed (**5,181,206 JS / 5,304,218 HTML characters; 2,253/2,253 Node tests plus Club26/FC27**). Focused gaze/blink/turn/shot visual suite **12/12**, Career/account-service **24/24**, UT lineup/API **48/48**, actual-renderer browser gaze QA, and `git diff --check` pass. In an ephemeral local PostgreSQL 16 container, service migrations/startup/health, competing-process refusal, lock release/reacquisition, terminated lock-session fail-closed behavior, Career no-result retry and durable-result replay, and concurrent cross-team UT idempotency each passed. This is local PostgreSQL integration evidence, not a customer-host/provider run. Candidate .49 SHA `0c73e6ce3415815d5599d7058588fefffd786c8c` passed exact-head Modular QA, Full Regression Census, Current Player Graphics QA, Pages deployment, and main Census. See [candidate .49 validation](CANDIDATE_012849_VALIDATION_20261105.md).

## Previous pushed candidate 0.12.81.47

Online fixed-tick callbacks now accept ordinary synchronous tick results as well as asynchronous persistence results. An unexpected timer-boundary exception/rejection closes only the affected match, clears its buffers and timer, frees participant slots, resets Career for retry, and never settles a result or reward. Operator fault logging is separately bounded to an opaque match ID and fixed code; neither exception details nor account data are included. Failed Career ticket creation attempts both durable lease release and local reservation cleanup.

Online Career fixture tickets now acquire a short, transactionally exclusive database lease bound to the authenticated participant and existing fixture match ID. Contending service instances cannot launch the same fixture concurrently; only a matching reservation ID can release the lease, and durable result progression clears it. Local fake-database race and expiry tests cover behavior; a real PostgreSQL concurrency/migration run remains open.

Existing procedural players now counter-lean the connected upper-body group around the waist during sharp turn transitions. The renderer passes its already-eased turn lag; the cue is bounded and mirrored by view side. It does not change legs, movement, collision, facing selection, or pose timers. Actual-renderer Chromium QA and visual inspection are available in the candidate validation evidence, but owner visual/device approval remains open.

Build passed (**5,178,419 JS / 5,301,431 HTML characters**); full Node passed **2,244/2,244** plus Club26/FC27. Focused online service/API/matchmaking/WebSocket/session suites passed **114/114**, release/version contracts **16/16**, and turn/shot renderer tests **8/8**. Production-renderer turn QA passed 40 frames across all eight headings with no page errors or gameplay state mutation; existing gait QA passed 288 frames. `git diff --check` passed. Candidate .47 SHA is `c14314ef9ce04709b4bbf7f3e6a77a8919e847ae`; exact-head dev Census, Modular QA, current-player graphics QA, protected promotion, Pages deployment, and main regression all passed. Real PostgreSQL lease migration/concurrency, public online configuration and physical two-device acceptance remain open. See [candidate .47 validation](CANDIDATE_012847_VALIDATION_20261105.md).

## Previous local candidate 0.12.81.46

Cup save slots now retain the exact persisted bytes loaded by the current tab. Before writing, a vault compares storage with that baseline; a changed value blocks the stale write, preserves the newer data, and keeps the stale tab's local copy as an exportable recovery snapshot. No schema changes or silent last-writer replacement are introduced.

Wet-pitch slides now add direction-aligned droplets and a steady water curl to the existing spray. Reduced-flash lowers opacity; the cue is attached only to live slide VFX, and gameplay state/physics are unchanged. Actual-renderer Chromium QA sampled dry, wet and reduced-flash cases with no page errors or state mutation.

After advancing all release/version/cache metadata, the final .46 build passed (**5,176,981 JS / 5,299,993 HTML characters**); full Node passed **2,231/2,231**, release/version contracts **22/22**, and the Club26/FC27 executable passed. Wet-spray contracts passed **3/3**, and actual-renderer Chromium QA passed dry/wet/reduced-flash samples with unchanged live state. Exact-head census passed after retry, Modular QA passed, protected promotion completed, Pages deployment passed, and main census passed. See [candidate .46 validation](CANDIDATE_012846_VALIDATION_20261105.md).

## Previous deployed candidate 0.12.81.45

An authoritative engine or snapshot failure now emits a fixed `{type:'match-error', code:'engine-error'}` event to both connected players. The client validates the event, clears that match's identity, rejects late snapshots, exits gameplay without calling the finished-result or reward path, and clears only the matching PVP retry marker. Career reports that the reserved fixture can be retried. Exception text and arbitrary failure reasons never cross the wire. Focused WebSocket/client/matchmaking/service tests passed **59/59**; mock browser QA covers PVP marker cleanup, no rewards, and the safe return route. A live-host failure drill remains open.

Existing in-game players also blink at deterministic, staggered idle intervals. Blink evaluation uses render time and player index only; hurt/action poses are preserved and player/ball simulation state remains unchanged. The new schedule tests passed **2/2**. Actual-renderer Chromium QA captured four frames, verified visible eyelid closure and reopening, and recorded no browser errors or state mutation. This is not owner visual/device acceptance.

Final .45 build passed (**5,174,947 JS / 5,297,959 HTML characters**), full Node passed **2,227/2,227**, release/version contracts **22/22**, and the Club26/FC27 executable passed. Focused online transport/client tests passed **59/59**, and idle-blink schedule tests **2/2**. Actual-renderer blink QA passed with four samples, reopened eyes, no page errors and unchanged state. An integrated online-account browser QA pass covered fault feedback and Career/UT flows; two later local reruns timed out at separate Career/UT navigation waits. After one unrelated `lan_auto_two_browser_qa.py` remote displacement timeout, the exact-head Census passed on same-SHA retry; Modular QA, protected promotion, Pages and main census passed. See [candidate .45 validation](CANDIDATE_012845_VALIDATION_20261105.md).

## Previous deployed candidate 0.12.81.44

Unexpected exceptions from the authoritative match engine's synchronous `step()` or post-step snapshot read are contained within the match session. Matchmaking closes/removes that match, clears its interval and pending input buffers, releases both account active slots, leaves no result to settle, and returns a bounded `engine-error` outcome instead of letting an exception escape the shared Node timer callback. Career fixtures return to the reserved state for retry. No client-specific crash message, live host failure drill, or result settlement is claimed.

Focused match-session and matchmaking tests passed **33/33**. ## Previous local candidate 0.12.81.34

PVP now has a bounded same-match recovery window after an unexpected transport loss: the server pauses simulation, a newly authenticated one-use ticket restores the disconnected user to their original side and latest authoritative snapshot, input sequence resets for that seat, and a grace timeout closes the match without settling a result. Intentional leave still exits immediately and Career keeps its scheduled-fixture retry. The classic gameplay ball now uses a curved, projected panel surface at the existing radius, rotation API, and physics. Online Ultimate Team's remote-squad reload control is wired to its actual loader. See [candidate .34 validation](CANDIDATE_012834_VALIDATION_20261104.md), [ball projection evidence](BALL_PANEL_PROJECTION_20261104.md), and [candidate .33 validation](CANDIDATE_012833_VALIDATION_20261104.md) for preceding Career/UT/server functionality.

Online Career is playable through the server: a signed-in user can start a server-owned eight-fixture season with an owned team, read fixtures and standings, and request the next pre-bound fixture. Only a matching durable result advances the season, idempotently; Career fixtures use server CPU while PVP pairs two human players. Online UT equips twelve unique canonical players in role-compatible slots; versioned, idempotent lineups are server-validated, ticket-bound and used by authoritative simulation. The local squad-copy workflow remains separate.

The authenticated result reward derives completion/win/draw tokens from immutable stored scores and commits capped balance plus an idempotent receipt. Local tests do not verify a live PostgreSQL database, configured OIDC provider, public network, multiple devices, horizontal/multi-process matchmaking, crash recovery, deployment or human visual/device acceptance. The connected Render workspace has an existing LAN signal service and no PostgreSQL database. A standalone Free account/match API Blueprint is in `render.online.yaml`; the existing `render.yaml` LAN service definition remains unchanged. The recommended closed-test stack is Render Free + Supabase Free Postgres + Auth0 Free OIDC. Render sleeps after 15 minutes without inbound traffic; Supabase Free has no automatic backups and may pause after a week of low activity. This is a prototype path, not durable production hosting. No Supabase project or Auth0 tenant has been provisioned; API database/OIDC settings and browser client configuration remain empty, so sign-in cannot safely be enabled until external accounts are configured. No real-money token purchase is enabled. Offline play and saves remain independent. See [online server deployment handoff](ONLINE_SERVER_DEPLOYMENT.md).

The match run cycle uses continuous stance/swing paths with support-foot contact, speed-scaled stride, knee/toe flex and a restrained torso counter-swing. See [run-cycle quality](PLAYER_RUN_CONTACT_QUALITY_20261104.md) and [gallery](PLAYER_RUN_CONTACT_GALLERY_20261104.png).

Candidate .81.12 adds an asymmetric near-leg kick/far-leg fold to the same launch pulse. The production-renderer gallery preserves nine distinct phases from launch through get-up and confusion, then checks the launch from all eight match headings (5,222+ painted pixels each); player/ball state remains unchanged. Focused tests pass 13/13 and Chromium reports zero page errors. Ball material lighting is now composited over the finished panel artwork, so the classic and optional balls retain dimensional shading across their panels instead of shading only the base sphere. Its ground shadow contracts and softens with ball height while preserving the exact grounded footprint and goalkeeper hold path. Match radius, rotation, physics and gradient-free fallback are unchanged. The focused ball/cosmetic suite passes 14/14; see [ball surface evidence](BALL_SURFACE_QUALITY_20261104.md) and [renderer comparison](BALL_SURFACE_QUALITY_20261104.png).

### 2026-11-23 — AI-THROUGH-BALL-LATERAL-LEAD-01 (smf-hourly-dev-20261019)
**Acceptance:** A CPU through ball must meet a committed receiver who is drifting laterally during flight, without changing the longitudinal run lead, exceeding the bounded correction, bypassing defender/keeper reads, or aiming outside the playable pitch.
**Implementation:** Project the receiver's perpendicular movement over the existing 0.12–0.35 s flight estimate, cap lateral lead at ±32 units, and clamp the final target to a 24-unit playable-field margin. Existing pass selection, ball physics and four actions remain unchanged.
**Regression:** Added mirrored Node coverage for lateral projection and cap/edge coverage; updated real-engine desktop/simulated-touch keeper acceptance to place the keeper at the projected target. Repaired the .227 browser fixture to use an existing lineup slot (1 rather than nonexistent slot 6). Exact .228 gates pending.


### 2026-11-23 — AI-SUPPORT-PASS-ARRIVAL-READ-01 (smf-hourly-dev-20261019)
**Acceptance:** Support-run PASS preference must evaluate the receiver where the existing PASS movement lead will meet them; a defender occupying that projected collection pocket cancels the preference, while a marker left behind by receiver movement does not. Unsafe primary support runners must not be reused by ordinary AI fallback when another teammate is available; no available alternative means retain possession.
**Implementation:** `attackingSupportPass` now projects the receiver with the same 0.12–0.35 s and ±65-unit lead as `GameEngine.pass`, then evaluates the projected lane and arrival pocket. `driveAI` excludes a rejected primary support runner from fallback and `pass()` honors that internal exclusion.
**Regression:** Updated the .228 flight-time assertion to measure longitudinal projection independently of lateral lead. Added mirrored Node and real-engine desktop/simulated-touch Chromium coverage for moving away from/into a marker and fallback exclusion. Exact .229 gates pending.

Defined the receiver-lead clamp locally inside attackingSupportPass; the prior helper was scoped only to attackingThroughBall. The .230 candidate retains projected-arrival defender checks and fallback exclusion. Exact-head Node/Chromium and Census verification pending.


### 2026-11-23 — AI-CUTBACK-SPECIALIST-RECEIVER-PRESERVATION-01 / AI-LOFTED-PASS-JUMP-INTERCEPT-01
**Acceptance:** A clear, independently validated cutback receiver must not be removed by the ordinary support-run fallback exclusion; the intended central runner receives the existing low PASS. Against a targeted lofted pass, the second CPU defender may use only the existing JUMP action when the projected ball crosses a reachable horizontal/vertical window after the difficulty reaction delay; low, untargeted, unreachable, too-late, airborne/recovering or human-controlled defenders do not trigger it, and the primary presser keeps pursuing the live ball.
**Implementation:** driveAI now preserves safe cutback/give-and-go/goalmouth/third-man/first-touch receivers when excluding an unsafe ordinary support runner. A bounded lofted-pass jump-window helper reuses the existing targeted-pass cover validation and checks projected defender/ball intersection, 24–132-unit height, 58-unit horizontal reach, 0.56-second horizon and ai.reaction before issuing jump().
**Regression:** Expanded tests/ai_cutback_pass.test.cjs to prove the same behind-the-carrier runner is rejected by ordinary support passing but remains the safe cutback target. Added mirrored helper/engine Node coverage and desktop/simulated-touch Chromium assertions that the second defender jumps while the primary continues pressing. Exact-head Modular QA and Full Regression Census pending; local clone was unavailable because github.com DNS did not resolve, so no local Node/Chromium run is claimed.
**.230 diagnosis:** Modular QA #45193483047 and Census #45193483239 failed on the same real-engine cutback assertion (jp3 selected instead of the independently validated jp1); .230 is not certified/promoted.


**.231 first exact-head QA correction:** Modular QA #45197195752 and Census #45197195929 each ran 2,811 Node tests (2,809 passed, 2 failed). The cutback gameplay regression passed. The lofted-pass engine test observed action `jump` but over-constrained the existing jump's initial z to greater than 10 (the current jump metric initializes at exactly 10); Node and desktop/simulated-touch browser assertions now verify `jumpCount===1`. The project-memory contract also caught the assigned checkpoint's missing Agent ID line, now restored. No production gameplay failure was established by these two assertions.



**.231 second exact-head diagnosis:** Census #45197700354 executed 2,811 Node tests (2,810 passed, 1 failed) because the assigned checkpoint rewrite shortened headings required by project_memory_contract.test.cjs. The cutback gameplay regression passed and the lofted-pass engine test observed the existing jump action; the checkpoint now restores Agent ID and all required sections. This is a memory-contract correction, not a production gameplay failure.


### 2026-11-23 — AI-LOFTED-PASS-JUMP-REACH-01 (smf-hourly-dev-20261019)
**Acceptance:** A CPU second defender may commit the existing JUMP only when its Jumping rating and predicted first-jump arc can reach the targeted lofted pass after the difficulty reaction window. A real airborne JUMP player must be able to collect a pass above the grounded 45-unit limit, while grounded players retain the old limit and a jumper cannot collect below its feet. Low-Jumping defenders, low/untargeted passes, stale ownership, unreachable timing and invalid states must fail closed.
**Implementation:** AI uses the shared JUMPMETRICS first-impulse/initial-lift values and the engine's 1,080-unit/s² player gravity to predict the defender's vertical envelope at the projected crossing. updateBall extends collection height only for a live `jump`/`doubleJump` state with positive jumpCount, using current player z and a bounded 18-unit lower-body tolerance. No pass trajectory, grounded reach, keeper reach, control, save, roster or persistence changes.
**Regression:** Added mirrored Node coverage for high/low Jumping ratings and actual GameEngine.updateBall airborne-vs-grounded possession; extended desktop/simulated-touch Chromium acceptance for both teams to verify real possession collection, four actions and no page errors. Initial preflight stopped on four README heading contracts before gameplay tests; metadata correction committed, exact-head Modular QA and Full Regression Census rerun pending.

### 2026-11-23 — AI-PRIMARY-PRESS-GOALWARD-CUTOFF-01 (smf-hourly-dev-20261019)
**Acceptance:** When a grounded opponent carrier is advancing toward its attacking goal, the primary CPU presser should cut across the next touch slightly farther down the attack axis instead of following only the carrier's exact velocity line. Retreating, lateral-only, stationary, invalid and zero-anticipation cases must retain existing behavior; the complete interception vector stays within the existing 34-unit cap.
**Implementation:** Add a bounded anticipation-scaled goalward term (maximum 8 units before the existing vector cap) to `defensiveCarrierLeadTarget`. It applies only above 80 units/s of goalward carrier velocity and is included in the same 34-unit vector clamp. No movement speed, tackle chance/contact, ball physics, human input, four actions, saves, rosters, persistence or offline behavior changes.
**Regression:** Added mirrored Node helper tests for forward, retreating, lateral-only and zero-read cases; strengthened the actual `driveAI` test to verify the exact goalward projection in both directions; desktop/simulated-touch browser acceptance continues to drive the real engine. Exact-head Modular QA and four-Node/four-Chromium Full Regression Census are pending.

### 2026-11-23 — AI-SLIDE-TACKLE-BALL-SIDE-READ-01 (smf-hourly-dev-20261019)
**Acceptance:** CPU SHOOT slide selection must target the live carried ball rather than merely the carrier's body. The ball must still be owned by the grounded carrier, lie within the existing 36-unit carried-ball envelope and be exposed on the defender-facing side. Far-side shielding, mismatched/unowned/stale/invalid ball positions cannot trigger a slide. Apply the same read to pressured first-touch slides; preserve existing run-up, facing, relative-motion, range and skill/difficulty/fatigue probability checks.
**Implementation:** Added an engine-level `defensiveTackleSlideBallSideRead` wrapper around the existing ball-side predicate and existing AI slide eligibility helper. ### 2026-11-23 — AI-SLIDE-TACKLE-BALL-AIM-01 (smf-hourly-dev-20261019)

### 2026-11-24 — KEEPER-FATIGUE-CATCH-THRESHOLD-01 (smf-hourly-dev-20261019)
**Acceptance:** Rested keepers retain the exact existing dive catch threshold. As fatigue rises, only borderline high-force shots should shift from a catch to a parry; the maximum threshold reduction is 10%. GK Skill/GK Diving still dominate, both goal directions behave identically, and invalid/out-of-range fatigue must fail safely. Dive reach/timing, parry direction, ball physics, controls, saves, rosters, persistence and offline play remain unchanged.

### 2026-11-24 — AI-GROUND-TRAP-JUMP-COUNTER-01 (smf-hourly-dev-20261019)
**Acceptance:** When an armed enemy Banana Peel or Ice Patch is immediately in a CPU outfielder's path and a safe SHOOT-slide sweep is unavailable, the CPU uses the existing JUMP action to evade it. A safe grounded slide still disarms the trap first. The first active JUMP frame must count as airborne before vertical lift reaches 20; a finished or non-jump action does not grant immunity. Carrying possession must be retained, and the CPU continues moving away while airborne until clear of the trap.
**Implementation:** reactToMagicHazard commits a bounded emergency JUMP only inside the immediate trap window after the safe-slide option fails, then keeps the jumper moving away. The shared Banana/Ice hazard model treats a live jump/doubleJump action with positive action time and jump count as an airborne counter on its first lift frame. Existing trap radius, arming, lifetime, slide disarm, hazard effects and four-button input remain unchanged.
**Regression:** Updated the stale keeper_parry_lane source contract to assert the fatigue-aware catchThreshold expression. Added Node tests for CPU jump dispatch, possession retention, both trap families, both teams, active/expired JUMP and slide priority. Extended actual-engine desktop/simulated-touch Chromium QA across both trap types, both goals and 30/60/120 Hz. The first .245 exact-head Node run found a test-only ReferenceError because the new hazard-model fixture referenced undefined carrier/field variables; the fixture now defines its own carrier and bounded field. Rerun at the new exact head pending.

### 2026-11-24 — AI-GROUND-TRAP-PATH-PRIORITY-01 (smf-hourly-dev-20261019)
**Acceptance:** When multiple armed enemy Banana Peel/Ice traps are within CPU awareness range, an already-overlapping trap is handled first; otherwise a trap intersecting the player's current travel corridor takes priority over a nearer off-lane trap. When no trap intersects the lane, nearest-distance fallback must remain unchanged. Preserve the existing reaction delay, safe SHOOT-slide priority, JUMP fallback, trap effects/radii/lifetimes, possession, four actions and save/offline compatibility.
**Implementation:** reactToMagicHazard computes each candidate's ahead/across projection from the current movement/facing heading, filters to the existing radius+60 awareness window, then chooses overlapping, in-lane (smallest bounded path score), or nearest fallback in that order. The selected candidate reuses the same corridor values for existing slide/JUMP decisions; no trap physics or input behavior changes.
**Regression:** Added mirrored Node and actual-engine desktop/simulated-touch Chromium cases for both teams with a nearer off-lane Banana and a farther in-lane Ice Patch; the AI must JUMP the in-lane trap. Existing single-trap, reaction-delay, slide-disarm, possession, 30/60/120-Hz and exactly-four-action checks remain. Exact-head Modular QA and four-Node/four-Chromium Census are triggered by the dev commit; no local Node/Chromium execution is claimed.

### 2026-11-24 — KEEPER-PARRY-REBOUND-CROSS-TRACK-READ-01 (smf-hourly-dev-20261019)
**Acceptance:** A CPU goalkeeper with substantial sideways momentum must not treat that cross-track velocity as if it were free forward acceleration when deciding whether to chase its own parried rebound. ### 2026-11-24 — AI-DRIBBLE-TRICK-READY-THREAT-01 (smf-hourly-dev-20261019)

Downed, invulnerable, shot-down/get-up, active hurt-recovery and shot-impact recovery states must not create false pressure or determine the escape side; an active slide remains a real threat. When a nearer opponent is unavailable, the next ready defender controls both the duel odds and escape direction. If no ready defender exists, the existing uncontested path remains. Preserve the human dribble-trick path, ten style profiles, costs/cooldowns, counterplay, ball physics, four actions, saves, rosters, persistence and offline behavior.
**Implementation:** GameEngine.tryDribbleTrick now filters unavailable opponents only for byAI attempts before selecting the nearest rival. The same selected active rival continues to drive the existing dribbleOdds contest and away-from-defender movement; human calls keep the previous selection behavior. No trick probabilities, style movement, fatigue, cooldown or contact thresholds changed.
**Regression:** Added mirrored actual-engine Node cases for downed, invulnerable, shot-down/get-up, hurt and shot-impact recovery states; active nearest defenders and active slides still challenge, no-ready-rival attempts retain the uncontested path, and human selection remains unchanged. Added desktop/simulated-touch Chromium acceptance for both teams, escape-side selection, 223 canonical teams and exactly four actions. Exact-head Modular QA and four-Node/four-Chromium Full Regression Census pending.


### 2026-11-24 — AI-PASS-INTERCEPTION-CROSS-TRACK-READ-01 (smf-hourly-dev-20261019)
**Acceptance:** The second CPU defender's early interception of a targeted grounded PASS must account for lateral momentum that still needs to turn into the ball lane. Momentum aligned with the target remains beneficial; perpendicular momentum must not be counted as free forward reach. Stationary movement and average Defence preserve their existing baseline. Keep the primary presser, receiver lead, existing 12 interception samples, 0.82 reach factor, +12 collection margin, pass trajectory, lofted-pass JUMP, tackle/contact resolution, four actions, saves, rosters, persistence and offline play unchanged.
**Implementation:** defensivePassInterceptionReachTarget now resolves the current velocity's cross-track component against each candidate lane point. The existing Agility-derived turn ramp estimates lateral drift; a bounded min(16, crossTrackSpeed * rampArea * 0.45) reserve is deducted before the existing reach factor. No player speed or ball physics is changed; only the support defender's early interception eligibility is refined.
**Regression:** Added mirrored Node cases for both directions proving aligned momentum reaches an earlier pass point than perpendicular momentum, and stationary movement is not less conservative than cross-track movement. Extended actual-engine desktop/simulated-touch Chromium QA with the same three movement profiles, while retaining Defence/Agility boundaries, primary pursuit, 223 canonical teams and exactly four action buttons. Exact-head Modular QA and four-Node/four-Chromium Census pending.

### 2026-11-24 — AI-CARRY-ESCAPE-PARALLEL-SLIDE-READ-01 (smf-hourly-dev-20261019)

## 2026-11-25 — AI-GROUND-PASS-KEEPER-RECOVERY-READ-01
CPU support, cutback, goalmouth and give-and-go ground-pass evaluators now use one shared keeper-availability predicate, matching the existing through-ball behavior. A keeper in `keeperDiveCatch`, `keeperRecover`, `keeperHold` or `keeperCatch` no longer suppresses a pass as an active blocker; idle and committed `keeperDive` states remain blockers. First-touch outlet logic continues to require actual active pressure. Mirrored Node and actual-engine desktop/simulated-touch Chromium regressions cover both directions and support/cutback/goalmouth routes. Full exact-head Modular QA and four-Node/four-Chromium Census are required before promotion.


### 2026-11-25 — AI-GROUND-PASS-HURT-RECOVERY-EXCLUSION-01 (smf-hourly-dev-20261019)

### 0.12.81.287 first exact-head QA diagnosis (25 November 2026)
Modular QA #45562171349 and Full Regression Census #45562171160 exposed one source-level integration error: the new high-cross turn-cost calculation referenced targetDx/targetDy without declaring them in keeperHighCrossClaimTarget. ### 27 November 2026 — HUMAN-EXPOSED-BALL-ARC-POKE-READ-01 (smf-hourly-dev-20261019)
**Acceptance:** For both defending teams, a short PASS poke must select and aim at the opponent carrier when the carrier's body center is just outside the existing 0.25 forward-arc threshold but the validated low/live/defender-facing ball itself enters that arc and remains inside the existing 130-unit short-poke reach. A shielded, stale, airborne, invalid or absent ball must not cause a carrier lunge; the existing nearest in-arc target or facing fallback remains. Preserve the 55 ms lead, 12-unit per-axis lead cap, 16-unit poke step, 62-unit contact check, cooldown, stamina, slide 220-unit reach/timing/travel, four actions, saves, rosters, persistence and offline play.
**Implementation:** GameEngine.tackle now uses the same validated defensivePokeBallSideRead for both short PASS pokes and SHOOT slides when an explicit live ball is supplied. The ball may select its owner only when it enters the forward arc and is within the existing action-specific reach (130 poke, 220 slide). Held-PASS release, close chase and Hide Ball counter-pokes now forward the current ball; calls without a live ball retain the prior body/facing fallback.
**Regression:** Extended tests/human_exposed_ball_slide.test.cjs with mirrored actual-GameEngine cases where the carrier body is outside the arc but the exposed ball is inside, plus shielded and missing-ball fallbacks and held-PASS/chase wiring. Extended tests/ai_defensive_poke_commit_browser_qa.py with desktop and simulated-touch actual-engine acceptance for both teams and all three cases. Updated tests/directed_pass_lane.test.cjs to match the current multiline tackle-target selection after the exposed-ball arc refactor; both target-priority assertions remain executable.
**.361 first exact-head QA diagnosis:** Modular QA #45977259080 ran 3,015 tests: 3,014 passed and one failed in the new short-PASS regression. The production poke released the ball on the expected exposed-ball vector; the fixture calculated its body-in-arc measurement after successful tackle resolution had pushed the carrier, so it measured the post-contact position rather than the pre-contact geometry. Node and browser fixtures now snapshot body/ball geometry before calling tackle. No production gameplay change was needed for this correction; exact-head reruns remain required.
**Release gate:** .361 exact-head Modular QA and all four Node/four Chromium Full Regression Census shards required. No promotion or deployment before green exact-head results.

**.364 exact-head fixture correction:** The first .364 validation exposed two test-only issues: the keeper recovery fixture accepted but did not apply its optional Agility profile, and `ai_defensive_poke_commit_browser_qa.py` retained instance-level `tackle`/`activateDefence` stubs from `runCloseContactPoke` and `runCpuSlide`, contaminating later first-touch airborne-boundary scenarios. The Node fixture now applies controlled GK stats and the browser scenarios restore prototype methods in `finally`; no production thresholds or assertions were weakened. Fresh exact-head Modular QA and all eight Census shards are required.

**.364 Agility-fallback assertion correction:** The first keeper Node regression correctly found that `STATS.stat()` infers a stable Agility value from legacy roster fields when the explicit field is absent or invalid; it does not force 50. The regression now checks missing/invalid parity and verifies the inferred result remains within the explicit low/high bounds. Production stat inference is unchanged.
